Motivation: 现有的医疗问答基准很少涵盖护士助产士所关注的孕产妇、新生儿、儿童及生殖健康问题，且缺乏公开的母幼健康指南检索相关性评估数据集。为此，作者希望构建更贴近实际临床需求的评估基准。

Method: 通过整合七个专家撰写的现有资源，构建包含25,949个条目的mamabench问答数据集，并设计多类型题型（选择题、简答、评分 rubric）；同时构建mamaretrieval检索数据集，将3,185个临床问题与63,650个文本块的相关性进行0-6分级标注，采用分解式评分标准区分‘真正回答问题’与‘仅涉及主题’的内容。强调使用专家来源、连续相关性评分以及对标签局限性进行量化评估（如范围分类一致性、前沿判断检查和池完整性审计）。

Result: 成功发布了mamabench和mamaretrieval两个新基准；重新调整了HealthBench的元评估以适配该领域；提出了一套更严谨的数据构建流程，包括非二元化标注和标签质量评估机制。

Conclusion: 这两个新基准填补了母幼健康领域自动问答与信息检索评估的空白，其构建方法强调透明度与现实适用性，有助于推动面向实际临床场景的医疗AI系统发展。

Abstract: Medical question-answering benchmarks rarely cover the maternal, neonatal, child, and reproductive-health questions a nurse-midwife asks, and, to our knowledge, no public chunk-level relevance benchmark exists for maternal-health guideline retrieval. We release two benchmarks that fill these gaps. mamabench is a scope-filtered QA set of 25,949 items assembled from seven existing expert-authored sources across multiple-choice, short-answer, and rubric-graded tracks; to help users calibrate the LLM judge that scores the rubric track, we re-scope HealthBench's physician-labelled meta-evaluation to the domain. mamaretrieval pairs 3,185 clinical queries with graded (0-6) relevance labels over a 63,650-chunk maternal-health guideline corpus, using a decomposed rubric that distinguishes a chunk that answers a query from one merely on its topic. Three decisions shape both: assemble and filter expert sources rather than author questions, grade relevance rather than binarise it, and measure and disclose the limits of the labels -- scope-classifier agreement, a frontier-judge check, and a pooling-completeness audit -- rather than treat them as an oracle. A companion paper uses the benchmarks to evaluate a deployed on-device assistant; both are released openly for research.

</details>


### [114] [To Reason or to Fabricate: Reasoning Without Shortcuts via Hint-Anchored Pairwise Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29481)
*Jiuheng Lin,Chen Zhang,Yansong Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HIPPO是一种新的强化学习框架，通过引入提示注入和配对奖励模型，有效区分真实推理与记忆捷径，提升大模型推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强化学习中因数据重叠导致模型依赖记忆而非真正推理的问题。

Method: 提出HIPPO框架，采用提示注入触发重叠行为，并利用配对比较和轻量级判断模型生成可区分的偏好信号。

Result: 实验表明HIPPO在多种任务上显著优于基线方法，并能泛化到分布外任务，证明其学到的是可迁移的真实推理能力。

Conclusion: HIPPO能有效抑制捷径依赖，促进大语言模型发展出稳健、可泛化的推理技能。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning (RL) significantly enhances LLM reasoning, its efficacy is severely undermined by Pre-RL data overlap, where RL datasets overlap with pretraining or SFT corpora, causing models to exploit shortcuts by memorizing correct answers and fabricating post-hoc reasoning. To address this, we introduce HIPPO, a novel RL framework that integrates hint-injected aggregation with a tailored pairwise reward model. By utilizing hint injection to deliberately trigger overlap-induced behaviors, the resulting traces naturally serve as explicit anchors for pairwise comparison. This provides highly discriminable preference signals, enabling a lightweight judge model to reliably distinguish genuine reasoning deduction from shortcut-driven rationalization, while the pairwise formulation ensures stable and robust optimization compared to standard PRMs. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HIPPO yields substantial improvements over standard baselines and generalizes effectively to out-of-distribution general tasks, showing it extracts authentic, transferable reasoning skills rather than superficial shortcut patterns.

</details>


### [115] [Which Tokens Need Context? A Reference-Based Analysis of Translation Responsibility Using Fertility and Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29489)
*Ramakrishna Appicharla,Baban Gain,Santanu Pal,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种模型无关的框架，通过词对齐衍生的生育率和熵来量化人类翻译中的上下文敏感性，发现上下文选择性地重新分配生成责任，而不改变整体生育率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解人类翻译中词汇对上下文依赖的差异，评估机器翻译系统是否以类人方式使用上下文。

Method: 提出一种事后、模型无关的框架，利用参考翻译数据和词对齐计算生育率与熵，分析不同词汇和句法层面的上下文敏感性。

Result: 上下文选择性地将生成责任从源词转移到上下文词，功能词生育率显著降低，内容词保持稳定；上下文主要用于消歧而非增加新信息。

Conclusion: 该框架为人类翻译中的选择性上下文使用提供了基于真实数据的刻画，可作为评估机器翻译模型的诊断基准。

Abstract: When humans translate, not every word depends equally on the surrounding context. Some tokens, particularly function words like pronouns and auxiliaries, rely heavily on preceding or following sentences, while others, such as proper nouns, do not. Understanding this inherent context sensitivity is essential for evaluating whether machine translation systems use context in human-like ways. However, existing approaches to analysing context usage rely on discourse-specific test sets or model internals, making them narrow or model-dependent. We propose a post-hoc, model-agnostic framework to quantify context sensitivity at lexical and syntactic levels using two measures derived from word alignments: fertility (number of target tokens generated per source token) and entropy (stability of fertility patterns across contexts). Using reference translations for three language pairs (German $\leftrightarrow$ English, English $\rightarrow$ Hindi) under four context conditions, we show that context selectively redistributes generative responsibility from source to context tokens without altering overall fertility. Function words show the largest fertility reductions, while content words remain stable, suggesting that context resolves ambiguity rather than adding new information. Our framework provides a ground-truth characterisation of selective context usage in human translation, establishing a diagnostic baseline for evaluating machine translation models.

</details>


### [116] [The Verbose Context Problem in Medical Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29503)
*Shiva Kaul,Min-Gyu Kim,Anjum Khurshid,Sriram Vishwanath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PopMedQA是一个用于解决人口健康领域中长文本上下文问题的基准测试，通过生成人工患者记录来评估语言模型在群体级纵向数据分析中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人群健康研究中，长期患者记录的分析通常涉及大量医学编码事件，导致文本表示冗长，超出语言模型处理能力，形成‘冗长上下文问题’。

Method: 提出PopMedQA基准，并利用新开发的neopatient库生成可控的人工患者记录，设计计算任务以评估模型性能；进行多种消融实验，包括提示策略、提示压缩和代理分解等方法。

Result: 实验表明，通用的域无关方法无法有效缓解冗长上下文问题，尤其是在处理超过40万token的病例数据时表现受限。

Conclusion: 需进一步利用领域特定结构来优化语言模型输入，以实现大规模人群健康推理。

Abstract: The verbose context problem occurs when structured concepts have token-inefficient textual representations. This bottleneck is acute in population health: cohort-level analysis of longitudinal patient records requires reasoning over thousands of medically-coded events, often exceeding 400K tokens in total. We present PopMedQA, a benchmark isolating this problem through computational tasks on groups of longitudinal patient records. We construct the benchmark using neopatient, a new library for language-controlled generation of artificial patient records. Through extensive ablations -- including prompting strategies, prompt compression, and agentic decomposition -- we find that domain-independent methods fail to alleviate the verbose context problem. There remains significant opportunity to exploit domain-specific structure in language model inputs for population-scale reasoning.

</details>


### [117] [Preference-ASR: A Preference-Aware Test Set for Benchmarking ASR in the Era of Speech LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29534)
*Nithin Rao Koluguri,Sasha Meister,Nikolay Karpov,Piotr Zelasko,Desh Raj,Jagadeesh Balam,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了PreferenceASR，一个用于评估ASR系统遵循用户自然语言偏好指令能力的新测试集，涵盖规范化、实体、不流利表达和大小写四个类别，并通过LLM辅助构建与人工验证确保质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR基准测试在数字、不流利表达、实体和大小写方面采用不一致的格式规范，且标准归一化方法抹去了用户关心的格式差异，无法评估模型对用户输出风格偏好的遵循能力。

Method: 提出PreferenceASR测试集，基于七个开源语料库，通过两阶段LLM辅助流水线并结合人工验证构建；设计偏好感知归一化器，在评估时根据当前指令选择性跳过归一化步骤。

Result: 对四个ASR模型的评测显示，不同偏好类型下模型排名发生变化，揭示了传统评估方法所掩盖的质量差异。

Conclusion: PreferenceASR能够有效衡量ASR系统对用户输出风格偏好的遵循能力，暴露传统指标无法发现的问题，为更贴近实际应用的ASR评估提供了新路径。

Abstract: Popular ASR test sets adopt inconsistent conventions for numbers, disfluencies, entities, and casing, while standard normalizers erase the format distinctions users care about. Current benchmarks therefore cannot measure whether a model follows user preferences for output style. We introduce PreferenceASR, a test set evaluating ASR systems on their ability to follow natural-language preference instructions across four categories: normalization, entities, disfluencies, and case. Built from seven open-source corpora via a two-stage LLM-assisted pipeline with human verification, it is evaluated with a preference-aware normalizer that selectively skips steps matching the active instruction. Benchmarking four models shows rankings shift across preference types, exposing quality differences traditional evaluation obscures. We publicly release the dataset.

</details>


### [118] [AURORA: Asymmetry and Update-Induced Rotation for Robust Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29545)
*Zishuai Zhang,Hainan Zhang,Zhiming Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AURORA，一种基于大语言模型参数梯度动态的新颖幻觉检测框架，通过捕捉幻觉与真实回答在梯度更新模式上的差异，在多个模型和数据集上实现了强健且可迁移的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法依赖高成本的输出一致性检查或静态隐藏状态探针，跨数据集表现差，难以捕捉深层语义不一致。因此需要一种更鲁棒、可泛化的检测机制。

Method: 提出利用权重梯度动态进行幻觉检测：1）计算权重矩阵与其梯度方向余弦相似度分布的偏度；2）通过SVD量化梯度更新对权重矩阵奇异向量基底的旋转程度（旋转比）。结合这两个特征实现检测。

Result: AURORA在四个模型家族和四个基准数据集上均取得优异的幻觉检测性能，具备良好的模型规模扩展性和跨域任务迁移能力，适用于数学推理和视觉-语言场景。

Conclusion: 基于梯度动态的分析为幻觉检测提供了新视角，AURORA通过捕捉参数更新中的结构不对称性，实现了高效、可泛化且无需额外微调的幻觉识别方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across a wide range of natural language processing tasks. However, their tendency to generate hallucinations, namely factually incorrect or unfaithful outputs, poses a critical obstacle to their deployment in high-stakes applications. Although recent hallucination detection methods have made encouraging progress, they typically rely on costly output-level consistency checks or static hidden-state probes that capture shallow dataset-specific patterns, leading to substantial degradation under cross-dataset evaluation. In this work, we propose AURORA, a novel hallucination detection framework that shifts the focus from static representations to the weight-gradient dynamics of LLMs. Our key insight is that hallucinated and faithful answers induce qualitatively different gradient update patterns on the model's parameters. Specifically, hallucinated samples trigger asymmetric and structurally misaligned gradients, which can be captured through two complementary features: (1) the skewness of the cosine similarity distribution between weight matrices and their gradient update directions, and (2) the rotation ratio, which quantifies how much the gradient update reorients the singular-vector basis of weight matrices via SVD. AURORA achieves strong hallucination detection performance across four model families and four benchmark datasets. Further analyses demonstrate that our method scales effectively across model sizes and transfers to out-of-domain tasks, including mathematical reasoning and vision-language scenarios.

</details>


### [119] [Coverage-Driven KV Cache Eviction for Efficient and Improved Inference of LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29563)
*Shuvendu Roy,Mengyao Zhai,Hossein Hajimirsadeghi,Golnoosh Samei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的KV缓存淘汰策略K-VEC，通过增强注意力头和模型层间的令牌覆盖率，在降低内存开销的同时有效缓解了大语言模型在长上下文任务中的性能下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存淘汰方法因减少唯一令牌覆盖而导致性能显著下降，尤其是在需要长上下文推理的任务中。本文旨在解决这一性能与内存权衡的问题。

Method: 提出K-VEC，引入跨头和跨层的覆盖率模块，优先保留能提升输入输出互信息的令牌，从而在淘汰过程中保持更高的令牌覆盖率。

Result: 在16个LongBench子集上评估显示，K-VEC在相同淘汰率和内存限制下比现有方法最高提升10.35分。

Conclusion: K-VEC通过覆盖感知的缓存淘汰机制有效平衡了内存效率与模型性能，为资源受限场景下的高效大模型部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel at complex tasks like question answering and summarization, thanks to their ability to handle long-context inputs. However, deploying LLMs is costly, not only due to the high computational demands of quadratic complexity of self-attention and auto-regressive generation, but also because of the significant memory overhead required for storing the key-value (KV) cache during inference. To reduce the memory cost, existing KV-cache eviction strategies leverage the sparsity in attention to selectively store a subset of tokens. While reducing the memory footprint, such approaches show a considerable drop in performance, especially in tasks that require long-context reasoning. We identify that the drop in performance is linked to a reduction in the coverage of unique tokens. Additionally, we theoretically show that reduced coverage limits the mutual information between inputs and outputs, thereby impairing predictive accuracy. To this end, we introduce K-VEC, a novel coverage-aware KV-cache eviction strategy that prioritizes token coverage while evicting tokens in the cache. K-VEC introduces a cross-head and a cross-layer coverage module to enhance token retention across attention heads and model layers, mitigating performance degradation caused by low coverage. Evaluated on 16 LongBench subsets, K-VEC exhibit up to 10.35 points improvement over the existing methods under the same eviction rate and memory constraint. Comprehensive evaluations validate the effectiveness of our approach and demonstrate its potential for efficient LLM deployment in resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [120] [Anisotropy Decides Cosine vs. Rank Metrics for Text Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29571)
*V. S. Raghu Parupudi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实验发现，文本嵌入相似性度量的选择取决于嵌入空间的几何特性：当嵌入分布各向同性时，余弦相似度最优；当存在显著各向异性（方差集中在少数方向）时，基于秩和L1的度量显著优于余弦相似度。一个关键指标——最大主成分所占方差比例——可高度预测替代度量的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有研究中对文本嵌入相似性度量选择不一致的问题，明确何种几何条件决定不同度量的优劣，并提供可解释、可预测的判断标准。

Method: 在19种无参数相似性度量、19个文本编码器（从小型句子变换器到七十亿参数大模型）和7个数据集上进行了全面的实证研究，并通过主成分分析和主导方向投影实验验证几何因素的因果作用。

Result: 发现嵌入空间的各向异性程度是决定相似性度量性能的关键：各向同性时余弦最优；各向异性时秩和L1类度量明显更优。最大主成分方差占比这一单一数值与替代度量增益高度相关（秩相关0.86，线性相关0.95）。投影去除主导方向后，仅在原为各向异性的模型上余弦性能恢复，验证了因果性。

Conclusion: 相似性度量的选择应基于嵌入空间的几何结构而非模型训练方式。余弦相似度在广泛使用的各向同性嵌入器中仍是最优选择，而各向异性时可由简单指标预判更优度量。提出一个单一位数诊断标准来指导实际应用中的度量选择。

Abstract: The standard way to compare two text embeddings is cosine similarity. Scattered studies report that a different metric does better, but never pin down the geometric condition that decides when, or why. We settle both with a comprehensive empirical study: nineteen parameter-free similarity metrics on nineteen encoders, from compact sentence transformers up to seven-billion-parameter large language models, across seven datasets. The answer is geometric. When an encoder spreads its variance evenly across directions, cosine is the best parameter-free choice and no other metric helps by a usable margin. When the variance concentrates into a few dominant directions, a property known as anisotropy, rank-based and L1-type metrics beat cosine by a clear margin. The absolute gain is modest, but because cosine starts low on these encoders it is a sizable relative improvement, around twenty percent on average and largest where cosine is weakest. What decides this is the geometry of the embedding space, not how the model was trained: where the two disagree, the metric follows the geometry. One number, the fraction of variance held by the single most dominant dimension, predicts how much the alternatives help across all nineteen encoders, with a rank correlation of 0.86 and a linear correlation of 0.95. To test this as the cause rather than a correlate, we project out the dominant directions: cosine recovers and the advantage of the other metrics nearly vanishes, but only on the encoders that were anisotropic to begin with. The effect is directional, not magnitude based, since it survives normalizing every vector to unit length. Among parameter-free metrics, then, cosine is the right tool wherever an encoder is well spread, which includes the fine-tuned embedders commonly deployed for retrieval, and we give a one-number diagnostic for when it is not.

</details>


### [121] [MAM-AI: An On-Device Medical Retrieval-Augmented Generation System for Nurses and Midwives in Zanzibar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29580)
*Yi Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MAM-AI是一个完全在安卓设备上离线运行的医疗问答助手，旨在支持桑给巴尔的助产护士，通过嵌入模型和生成模型结合指南文档提供带引用的回答，系统在检索方面表现良好，但小规模生成模型在帮助性和安全性之间存在权衡，最终通过优化提示提升实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 撒哈拉以南非洲地区孕产妇和新生儿死亡率居高不下，助产护理常由缺乏国际标准培训的护士提供，且临床现场难以访问权威指南（文档冗长、网络不稳定），因此需要一种可在资源受限环境下可靠运行的本地化智能辅助工具。

Method: 开发MAM-AI系统：使用3亿参数的EmbeddingGemma模型进行问题嵌入，并与87份指南文件（共63,650段）构成的本地知识库匹配；再由40亿参数的Gemma 4B int4生成模型在无网络条件下生成带引用的回答；整个系统部署于普通安卓设备，评估采用分层方法（检索器、理想上下文下的生成器、端到端性能及延迟），并使用经医生评分标准验证的LLM裁判进行打分。

Result: 在设备上的检索基本解决：3亿参数嵌入模型在七种检索器中排名第三，接近云端系统水平；但小型生成器存在问题——引入检索上下文未能提升回答质量，且在40亿参数规模下无法同时保证帮助性与安全性：两个同规模模型中，更“有帮助”的版本会出现真实危险错误，因此选择更忠实于来源的版本（忠实度堪比前沿大模型），并通过重新设计提示词将回避问题的比例从33%降至3%；知识库质量至关重要——当库中包含正确段落时回答具体可行，否则趋于模糊。

Conclusion: MAM-AI是一个经过全面评估的开源研究原型，证明了在资源受限环境中本地化医学问答的可行性，其核心挑战已从检索转移到生成模型的能力限制，尤其在小模型上实现安全与有用的平衡仍需突破，高质量知识库对系统表现具有决定性影响。

Abstract: Maternal and newborn mortality remain among the highest in sub-Saharan Africa, where midwifery care is often delivered by nurses who lack midwifery training to international standards, and consulting authoritative guidance at the point of care is hard: the guidelines are long and connectivity is intermittent. We present MAM-AI, a medical question-answering assistant for nurse-midwives in Zanzibar that runs entirely on a commodity Android device: a question is embedded (EmbeddingGemma, 300M) and matched against a curated corpus of 87 guideline documents (63,650 passages), then answered with citations by a 4B int4 generator (Gemma 4 E4B), fully offline, with no query leaving the device. We evaluate the exact deployed configuration with a layered methodology -- retriever, generator under oracle context, end-to-end, and latency -- scored by LLM judges validated against physician rubrics. The evaluation relocates the hard problem. On-device retrieval is essentially solved: the 300M embedder ranks third of seven retrievers and rivals cloud systems, so the passages the system needs are usually found. The small generator is what remains in doubt: adding retrieved context does not improve its answers, and at 4B it cannot be both helpful and safe at once -- of two same-size candidates, the more helpful one commits genuine dangerous errors, so we deploy the other, which is about twice as faithful to its sources (as faithful as a frontier model), and recover its helpfulness with a redesigned prompt that cuts deflection from 33% to 3%. Corpus quality is decisive for the same reason: where the corpus holds the right passage the answer is specific and actionable, and where it does not it goes vague. MAM-AI is a thoroughly evaluated, open-source research prototype, not a fielded product; the system, knowledge base, benchmarks, and evaluation harness are released.

</details>


### [122] [How much of an LLM-generated clinical corpus is actually new? A production-scale measurement of content redundancy for provenance classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29605)
*Ali H. Lazem,William J. Teahan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了由大语言模型生成的临床数据集中的冗余问题，发现原始token数量高估了信息量近九倍，其中79.4%为冗余内容；通过溯源分析和无模型压缩方法识别冗余机制，并证明去重后能提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着临床机器学习越来越多地依赖大语言模型生成的数据而非临床医生标注数据，仅以数据规模衡量其价值可能误导研究进展，因此需要评估生成数据中实际的信息含量。

Method: 提出“基于溯源的冗余分解”方法，对多智能体临床提取管道产生的25.1亿个token进行逐token溯源分类，并结合无模型的无损压缩分析验证结果；同时在下游任务中测试去重前后对临床编码器性能的影响。

Result: 仅有10.9%的输出为可训练的唯一内容，79.4%为冗余；识别出两种冗余机制：源文本复制和跨记录生成文本重复，前者可无损去除；一个通道几乎无冗余，表明冗余程度与结构设计相关；去重后在相同token预算下提升了外部疾病识别基准的表现。

Conclusion: 单纯依赖生成数据的规模会严重高估其信息价值，未纠正的冗余会扭曲训练分布并损害模型性能；应重视数据生成过程中的结构性冗余问题，并在训练前进行有效去重以提高效率和效果。

Abstract: Clinical machine learning increasingly relies on training corpora generated by large language models (LLMs) rather than annotated by clinicians, and such corpora are described and reused largely on the basis of their reported scale. We test whether volume reflects information content. Analysing the complete output of a multi-agent clinical extraction pipeline applied to 167,034 patient narratives, 2.51 billion generated tokens across the ten text-bearing channels of an eleven-channel pipeline, we introduce Provenance-based Redundancy Decomposition, a token-level classification of the entire output by source. Only 10.9% of the output is trainable-unique content while 79.4% is redundant; raw token count overstates information content by roughly ninefold. The redundancy arises through two distinct mechanisms, verbatim copying of source context into per-item fields, and duplication of generated text across records, of which only the former is losslessly removable. An independent, model-free analysis based on lossless compression confirms the redundancy, recovering the two mechanisms without reference to the provenance labels. One pipeline channel carries almost no redundancy, showing that the level of redundancy depends on how each channel is structured rather than being a fixed property of LLM extraction. Because uncorrected redundancy up-weights the longer, more complex presentations that generate the most items, it skews the token-level training distribution of the corpus, a property we measure directly. In a controlled downstream test, de-duplicating the corpus before adaptation improved a clinical encoder on external disease-recognition benchmarks at equal token budget, robustly across adaptation depths and replicated on a second benchmark, confirming that the redundancy carries a measurable cost beyond storage. The classification tool is released openly.

</details>


### [123] [Do We Still Need Fine Tuning? Turkish Sentiment Analysis in the Era of Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29614)
*Sercan Karakaş,Yusuf Şimşek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在土耳其语情感分析中，微调的BERTurk模型在三类任务中表现最佳，提示的大语言模型在包含中性类时性能下降，表明监督微调仍有必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在大语言模型时代，土耳其语情感分析是否仍需监督微调。

Method: 比较经典机器学习方法、微调的预训练语言模型和提示的大语言模型在土耳其语电商评论数据集上的表现。

Result: 微调的BERTurk模型在完整三类任务中表现最好，提示的大语言模型在二分类任务中较有竞争力，但在三类任务中因将中性评论极化而性能下降。

Conclusion: 在现实的土耳其语情感分类中，提示的大语言模型在零样本设置下尚未达到监督微调的效果，且包含中性类对稳健评估至关重要。

Abstract: This study examines whether supervised fine-tuning remains necessary for Turkish sentiment analysis in the era of large language models. We compare classical machine learning methods, fine-tuned pretrained language models, and prompted large language models on a Turkish e-commerce review dataset with negative, neutral, and positive labels. Fine-tuned BERTurk models perform best overall and outperform all prompted large language models in the full three-class task. The neutral class emerges as the main difficulty: while several large language models are much more competitive in binary positive--negative classification, they degrade substantially in the three-class setting by collapsing neutral reviews into polarized categories. The findings suggest that, in realistic Turkish sentiment classification, prompted large language models do not yet match supervised fine-tuning in the zero-shot setting, and that including the neutral class is crucial for robust evaluation.

</details>


### [124] [Two-Stage Prompt Optimization for Few-Shot Relation Extraction: From Reasoning-Guided Search to Gradient-Guided Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29639)
*Aunabil Chakma,Mihai Surdeanu,Eduardo Blanco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合基于推理和基于梯度的两阶段自动提示优化框架，用于小规模语言模型的少样本关系抽取，取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在小规模语言模型上，针对情景式少样本关系抽取任务，自动提示优化仍缺乏探索。

Method: 设计了一个两阶段框架：第一阶段使用基于推理的提示优化方法进行广泛优化；第二阶段引入GradPO方法，利用损失和梯度信号识别高影响提示片段并进行局部编辑优化。

Result: 在FS-TACRED和FS-FewRel数据集上实验表明，局部优化能提升第一阶段的提示效果，且GradPO是最稳定的优化器；在Qwen3-4B模型上于FS-TACRED达到SOTA，在FS-FewRel上表现具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段提示优化框架有效提升了小规模语言模型在少样本关系抽取任务上的性能，尤其GradPO作为一致性强的优化器具有应用价值。

Abstract: Automatic prompt optimization is still underexplored for episodic few-shot relation extraction with smaller language models. We propose a two-stage framework that combines reasoning-based prompt optimization with gradient-based prompt optimization. The first stage can use any reasoning-based optimizer to make broadprompt improvements in natural language. The second stage applies our GradPO, which uses loss and gradient signals to identify high-impact prompt spans and refine them with local edits. Experiments on FS-TACRED and FS-FewRel show that local refinement usually improves prompts found by the first stage, and GradPO is the most consistent refiner. Our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance on FS-TACRED with Qwen3-4B and remains competitive on FS-FewRel.

</details>


### [125] [Hybrid Retriever Evolution for Multimodal Document Reasoning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29648)
*Bohan Yao,Shruthan Radhakrishna,Vikas Yadav*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于失败驱动演化的元代理框架，用于在多模态文档问答中自主学习如何协调不同类型的检索器（如词法、语义和多模态），使检索调度成为可学习的推理过程。该方法通过分析错误推理路径并迭代优化任务代理的指令，实现了自适应的检索路由与跨模态证据组合，在多个基准上显著超越现有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统通常以固定流水线方式组合不同检索器，难以适应多步推理中动态变化的需求，缺乏对何时使用何种检索器以及如何融合结果的灵活决策能力。

Method: 设计一个元代理，通过分析任务代理的错误推理轨迹，主动探测工具环境以诊断根本原因，并迭代重写任务代理的指令，从而实现对多种检索器的自适应调度与协同。

Result: 在MMLongBench-Doc和DocBench上，进化后的代理相比基线最多提升+19.6分，且优于MACT、MDocAgent和SimpleDoc等最新系统；消融实验表明性能提升源于自适应路由与证据组合，而非依赖单一预设检索模式。

Conclusion: 自主的多智能体协同可作为多模态文档推理的一种有前景范式，将检索调度从固定的前端步骤转变为动态、可学习的推理组成部分。

Abstract: Different retrievers, including lexical, semantic, and multimodal approaches, provide highly complementary strengths for multimodal document understanding, yet most systems combine them through fixed pipelines that cannot adapt to the demands of individual reasoning steps. In this work, we ask whether retrieval orchestration itself can be learned as part of the reasoning process. We introduce a failure-driven evolution framework in which a meta-agent autonomously discovers how a tool-using task agent should coordinate diverse retrievers during multi-step document question answering. The meta-agent analyzes incorrect reasoning trajectories, actively probes the same tool environment to diagnose root causes, and iteratively rewrites the task agent's instructions, turning retrieval from a fixed front-end stage into an adaptive, step-wise reasoning decision. The evolved agent learns when to invoke each retriever, how to combine them, and how to compose evidence across modalities and pages. On MMLongBench-Doc and DocBench, the evolved agent achieves gains of up to +19.6 points over the unevolved baseline and consistently outperforms recent systems including MACT, MDocAgent, and SimpleDoc. Detailed retrieval analyses confirm that these improvements arise from adaptive routing and evidence composition rather than reliance on any hard coded retrieval mode, and evolution dynamics reveal a progressive shift from narrow lexical behavior to rich multi-tool coordination. These findings establish autonomous multi-agent coordination as a promising paradigm for multimodal document reasoning.

</details>


### [126] [Resolution Thresholds in VLM Detection of Harmful ASCII Art Across Construction Modes and Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29649)
*Yikai Hua,Peter West*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了图像分辨率对大型视觉语言模型（VLM）检测有害ASCII艺术文本能力的影响，发现在特定分辨率阈值以上检测率显著下降，尤其是基于单词的字符构造模式最难被检测，揭示了当前VLM内容审核系统的系统性漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于VLM在内容审核中易受将有害文本编码为ASCII艺术图像的越狱攻击，本研究旨在探究图像分辨率如何影响其检测性能，以识别并缓解这一安全漏洞。

Method: 通过构建一个生成十种不同分辨率ASCII艺术图像的管道，在八种字符构造模式（L1-L8）下评估八个最先进的VLM在英汉语料库上的表现，分析其检测失败的规律。

Result: 检测率在超过某一分辨率阈值后急剧下降；基于单词的构造模式在整个分辨率范围内最难以被检测；该现象在不同模型、语言和构造模式中具有一致性。

Conclusion: VLM在高分辨率下对特定形式的ASCII艺术攻击存在系统性检测盲区，建议建立考虑分辨率因素的内容审核评估标准。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as content moderation tools, yet they remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks in which harmful text is visually encoded as ASCII art. This can allow inappropriate or harmful content to bypass moderation systems. To address this vulnerability, this paper investigates how image resolution affects VLM detection of harmful ASCII art across eight character construction modes (L1-L8), ranging from dense block characters to word-embedded designs. We evaluate eight state-of-the-art VLMs on English and Chinese corpora using a pipeline that generates ASCII art images at ten resolution scales, probing whether a consistent detection-failure threshold exists across models, modes, and languages. Results indicate that detection rates decline sharply above certain resolution thresholds, and that word-based modes are the most resistant to detection across the full resolution range. These findings reveal a systematic vulnerability in VLM-based content moderation systems and motivate resolution-aware evaluation standards.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Recursive Self-Evolving Agents via Held-Out Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28374)
*Michael Nguyen,Quoc Nguyen,Paul Vuong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过自然语言工件（如策略、技能和流程）改进LLM代理的方法，提出了递归自进化代理RSEA，并在多个基准上进行了评估，发现其方法在保持性能不退化的同时实现了最佳表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自然语言工件提升LLM代理性能的方法通常只在单一基准上报告结果，缺乏统一比较；本文旨在系统性地评估这些方法，并提出更安全、有效的自我进化机制。

Method: 提出RSEA（递归自进化代理），维护一个三层自然语言状态（策略、技能、流程），通过轨迹递归重写这三层，并使用独立验证集上的严格保留-更好门控机制来防止性能退化。

Result: 在四个基准（ALFWorld, GAIA, (τ)-bench, WebShop）和六个基线上测试表明：1）没有一种工件在所有任务上都占优；2）无保护的上下文演化具有高方差且不安全；3）RSEA通过严格的保留机制确保性能单调提升，从不显著低于基线。RSEA在ALFWorld上达到69.3%（优于ReAct的64.6%），重试后达79.4%，为最佳结果。

Conclusion: RSEA通过结构化的自然语言状态和严格的验证机制，实现了安全、稳定的自我进化，是当前最有效的单次推理方法之一，尤其在复杂环境中表现出色且可靠。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly improved without weight updates by evolving a natural-language artifact, such as reflections, workflows, playbooks, cheatsheets, or optimized prompts, that conditions a frozen policy. Such methods are typically reported as wins on the single benchmark where they help. We study them apples-to-apples and surface a sharper picture. We introduce RSEA, a Recursive Self-Evolving Agent that carries a compact three-layer natural-language state: an imperative strategy, reusable skills, and a procedural playbook. Across generations, RSEA rewrites all three layers from its own trajectories and commits a candidate only if it does not regress on a disjoint held-out split, using a strict keep-better gate.
  Across four diverse benchmarks, ALFWorld, GAIA, (τ)-bench, and WebShop, and six faithful baselines, ReAct, Reflexion, GEPA, AWM, ACE, and Dynamic Cheatsheet, all evaluated on one shared local backbone, we find three main results. First, no artifact universally wins. RSEA is the strongest single-pass method on ALFWorld, reaching 69.3% compared with 64.6% for ReAct (McNemar (p=0.015)), and reaches 79.4% with retry, the best overall result. However, concrete-workflow induction, represented by AWM, is best on the strong-backbone tool-use tasks. Second, unguarded context evolution is high-variance and unsafe. Dynamic Cheatsheet, which curates context online without a held-out gate, is near-best on ALFWorld at 70.7%, yet collapses on WebShop, with a score of 0.14 compared with 0.43 for ReAct. Third, RSEA's strict held-out selection is what makes recursive self-evolution monotone-safe: it never significantly underperforms the base agent on any benchmark and falls back to vanilla ReAct when evolved context would hurt.

</details>


### [128] [Data and Evaluation Closed-Loop for Model Capability Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28471)
*Zhixuan Li,Jiangan Yuan,Han Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“能力切片”（capability slice）的概念，通过细粒度的评估单元将模型表现失败映射到具体的数据干预，形成从评估到数据优化的闭环，实现了可审计、可复现的模型能力诊断与改进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型预训练中，模型能力无法直接观测，评估结果滞后且模糊，工程上难以准确推断出数据层面的修复策略。作者希望弥合评估与数据之间的语义鸿沟，使能力缺陷的归因和数据优化变得系统化而非依赖直觉。

Method: 提出“能力切片”作为评估的基本单元，结合评估分类法、非指令数据分类法及映射规则，构建一个从基准测试失败到针对性数据干预的闭环系统，并通过两个反向案例验证该方法的有效性。

Result: 在两个相反的案例中，该方法均能做出正确判断：一是发现BBH性能下降源于<EOS>掩码损失问题，无需修改数据即可恢复性能；二是针对数学推理弱点设计采样策略，使AIME2025/AIME2026 Pass@128从6.67/0.00提升至26.67。

Conclusion: 评估到数据的推理可以成为常规化、可审计且可验证的过程，而非依赖工程师直觉，为模型能力优化提供了系统性路径。

Abstract: Model capability is the central variable in LLM pre-training, yet is never observed directly: data shapes it prospectively, while evaluation reveals it only retrospectively, compressing samples, prompts, decoding, and scoring rules into one noisy score. Practical optimization runs this backward: a failure is observed first, and the engineer must infer the corpus fix. The two sides speak incompatible vocabularies -- benchmark names and per-sample correctness versus data sources, domains, and quality labels -- so this inference is usually intuition, not method. We close this gap with the \emph{capability slice}: a group of evaluation samples sharing background condition, task type, solving operation, and output constraint -- precise enough to localize a single weakness yet stable enough to survive aggregation, unlike a benchmark name, too coarse, or a single sample, too noisy. Built around this unit, an evaluation taxonomy, a non-instruction data taxonomy, and mapping rules form a closed loop turning a benchmark-level failure into a targeted, testable data intervention. We test this loop on two case studies pulling in opposite directions. First, the loop rules the data out: continued pre-training drives BBH down by $-46.82\%$, but diagnosis traces this to a single masked \texttt{\textless EOS\textgreater} loss rather than weakened reasoning; restoring it recovers BBH to $66.44$, above the original checkpoint, without changing the data. Second, the loop rules the data in: a persistent math-reasoning weakness is decomposed by solving operation into specific failing combinations, and a weakness-targeted sampling procedure built from it lifts AIME2025/AIME2026 Pass@128 from $6.67$/$0.00$ to $26.67$ each. The same unmodified loop reaches opposite, correct verdicts in both cases, showing the evaluation-to-data inference can be routine, auditable, and experimentally validated rather than intuitive.

</details>


### [129] [GPTNT: Benchmarking Real-Time Collaboration Between Multimodal Agents on Keep Talking And Nobody Explodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28514)
*Amit Parekh,Sabrina McCallum,Kareem Al-Hasan,Malvina Nikandrou,Alessandro Suglia,Ioannis Konstas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GPTNT是一个基于合作视频游戏的基准测试，用于评估多模态模型在实时协作、信息不对称和时间压力下的沟通与协作能力，揭示了现有模型在状态跟踪、时间压力下行动效率等方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态模型基准通常孤立地研究协作条件（如时间压力、信息不对称），缺乏综合这些因素的真实协作环境评估，因此需要一个更贴近实际协作挑战的基准。

Method: 构建基于游戏Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes的基准GPTNT，其中两个代理分别拥有视觉/操作权限或指令信息，必须通过异步实时通信协作拆弹；通过隐藏手册、伙伴或两者来分离协作与记忆依赖，并利用程序化生成和玩家社区实现持续演化。

Result: 当前最先进的闭源和开源模型均未能在实时环境中成功拆除任何一个炸弹（而人类玩家可以做到），实验发现模型在状态跟踪、时间压力下的高效行动、处理歧义和错误恢复方面存在严重缺陷。

Conclusion: GPTNT为多模态模型提供了一个动态、真实且可持续演化的协作性能评估基准，揭示了现有系统在复杂协作任务中的关键弱点，推动未来研究关注实时协作能力的发展。

Abstract: Multimodal models are increasingly deployed to solve tasks collaboratively with humans or other artificial agents. Existing benchmarks show that these models possess many of the required component capabilities, but the conditions that coincide in collaboration, including time pressure, information asymmetry, and imperfect communication, are usually studied in isolation. We introduce GPTNT, a benchmark built on the cooperative video game Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes, in which two agents must coordinate to defuse procedurally generated bomb puzzles against a live countdown. One agent can see and manipulate the bomb but does not have the defusal instructions; the other has the instructions but cannot see or manipulate the bomb. Neither agent can succeed alone: success requires effective and efficient communication. Unlike turn-based proxies, GPTNT requires agents to act asynchronously and communicate in real time. GPTNT is designed to separate collaboration from reliance on memorized solutions: the instruction manual, the partner, or both can be withheld to isolate what a model derives in the moment from what it already knows. We show that GPTNT poses a substantial challenge for state-of-the-art systems: none of the closed- or open-source models we test defuses a single bomb in real time, a bar that human players clear. Through controlled experiments, we identify critical weaknesses in state tracking, efficient action under time pressure, ambiguity handling, and error recovery. We release GPTNT as a benchmark for collaborative performance that current evaluations leave unmeasured. Because it runs on the real game, GPTNT benefits from procedural generation and inherits a living modding community, allowing the benchmark to evolve as models improve rather than being solved once and retired.

</details>


### [130] [IMCBench: A benchmark for multimodal LLMs in Image-grounded Medical Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28556)
*Maria Xenochristou,Ashutosh Joshi,Korosh Vatanparvar,Mohammad Abuzar Hashemi,Prasad Kasu,Deepak Bansal,Anchal Nema,Nivedita Wadhwa,Prashams S Jain,Rebecca Abraham,Will Kimbrough,Dilek Hakkani-Tur,Wilko Schulz-Mahlendorf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IMCBench是一个图像支持的多轮医学对话基准，用于评估临床AI模型在安全性、准确性和不确定性使用方面的表现，揭示了现有模型在不同维度上的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医学AI基准存在碎片化问题，缺乏同时支持多轮对话和多模态输入的综合评估框架，因此需要构建更贴近真实临床场景的评测基准。

Method: 提出IMCBench，结合真实的临床图像与合成患者档案，构建多轮医学对话数据集，并从安全、准确和不确定性使用三个维度，采用LLM-as-Jury方法对标专家标注对八个前沿多模态模型进行评分。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6得分最高（3.61），其次是Claude Sonnet 4.6和GPT-5.2；但所有模型在恶性病和罕见病情况下安全性均下降（Δ = -0.27），且移除视觉输入或EHR信息会导致安全评分显著降低。

Conclusion: 准确的临床描述并不等同于安全的患者指导，必须通过多维度评估框架来推动医疗AI的安全可靠发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models and vision-language models have enabled reasoning over multimodal data, offering opportunities for clinical applications such as decision support and triaging. However, existing medical AI benchmarks are fragmented: some support multi-turn dialogues but lack images, while others provide multimodal inputs but focus on single-turn QA tasks. To address this gap, we introduce IMCBench, an image-grounded, multi-turn medical conversation benchmark that pairs real, publicly available clinical images with synthetic patient profiles to simulate realistic patient-clinician interactions. Each conversation is evaluated across three clinical dimensions: safety, accuracy, and appropriate use of uncertainty in diagnosis. We benchmark eight multimodal frontier models across four model families (Claude, GPT, Nova, and Llama), scoring each on a 1-5 scale using LLM-as-Jury scoring calibrated against expert clinician annotations. Our results show that Claude Opus 4.6 achieves the highest overall score (3.61), followed by Claude Sonnet 4.6 (3.30) and GPT-5.2 (3.29), though no model dominates all dimensions and safety degrades for both malignant and rare conditions ($Δ$ = -0.27 each). Ablation studies further reveal that both visual input and EHR context contribute to safe guidance (safety drops of 0.18 and 0.23 on average when each is removed), with stronger models leveraging visual features more effectively. Together, these findings demonstrate that accurate clinical description does not guarantee safe patient guidance, motivating the need for multi-dimensional evaluation frameworks in medical AI.

</details>


### [131] [Search for Truth from Reasoning: A Dynamic Representation Editing Framework for Steering LLM Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28589)
*Tianlong Wang,Yuhang Wang,Weibin Liao,Xin Gao,Xinyu Ma,Yang Lin,Yasha Wang,Liantao Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DynaSteer的动态表示编辑框架，通过分析推理链中的真理几何结构，结合模式聚类与Fisher-LDA方法，在关键不确定性节点进行精准干预，有效提升了大语言模型的推理准确性，并展现出良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提升大语言模型推理能力的方法（如思维链和“等待”提示）主要鼓励模型更多思考，但缺乏对“真理”的引导；而表示编辑（RepE）虽能内在控制模型行为，却未充分应用于动态推理过程。因此，需要探索如何在动态推理路径中有效引导模型走向正确答案。

Method: 通过研究展开式推理链中“真理”的几何特性，发现其在句子级别编码且与潜在推理模式纠缠；提出DynaSteer框架：利用模式聚类分离推理流形，采用Fisher-LDA提取纯净的真理方向，并基于前瞻熵动态监测，在高不确定性的早期分支点进行选择性干预与回滚。

Result: 在多个MATH基准测试上验证了DynaSteer的有效性，显著提升了推理准确率；在跨领域的编程任务上也表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DynaSteer通过动态、精准的表示编辑，在不破坏正确推理路径的前提下有效引导模型走向真理，为增强大语言模型的推理能力提供了新思路。

Abstract: Current approaches to enhance Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning, such as Chain-of-Thought and "Wait" prompts, primarily encourage models to think more, yet often fail to guide them toward Truth. While Representation Editing (RepE) offers a intrinsic control, its application to dynamic reasoning trajectories remains underexplored. In this work, we bridge this gap by investigating the geometry of truth within unfolding reasoning chains. We uncover three critical insights: (1) Truth is encoded at the sentence level and is entangled with latent reasoning patterns; (2) Effective intervention follows an Uncertainty Principle and a Decay Effect, requiring localization to early, high-entropy forks; (3) Naive steering vectors suffer from noise, risking collateral damage to correct trajectories. Based on these findings, we propose DynaSteer, a dynamic RepE framework. DynaSteer employs pattern clustering to disentangle reasoning manifolds and utilizes Fisher-LDA to project purified truth. By dynamically monitoring lookahead entropy, it selectively steers and rolls back trajectories only when necessary. Comprehensive experimental results on several MATH benchmark verify the effectiveness of DynaSteer, and experiments on out-of-domain coding tasks further confirm its generalization ability. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/tianlwang/DynaSteer.

</details>


### [132] [Aristotelian Virtue Profiling of LLMs through Ethical Dilemmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28683)
*Ioannis Tzachristas,John Pavlopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了VirtueMap框架，用于通过亚里士多德德性伦理学视角分析大语言模型在伦理困境中的响应模式，通过人类或LLM对五种回应进行排序，并基于共识建立评分标准，评估结果显示不同LLM在勇气、节制和正义等德性上表现差异较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在面对伦理权衡时可能表现出不同的价值观优先级，缺乏系统性评估方法，因此需要一种能够量化模型在多种德性维度上行为的框架。

Method: 提出VirtueMap框架，针对七个非致命、非政治、非宗教的伦理困境，为每种德性定义五种回应的排序，并通过超过100名受访者的评估验证排序一致性，仅保留95%以上确认的排序作为基准；使用归一化Borda对齐方法对LLM的排名进行评分，生成五大德性（实践智慧、正义、真实、勇气、节制）上的特征谱型。

Result: 在九个LLM家族上应用VirtueMap，发现平均排名一致性高达90.3%，最大差异出现在勇气、节制和正义维度；同时发布了一个可在浏览器中本地计算并比较个体与LLM德性谱型的交互式网站。

Conclusion: VirtueMap为评估大语言模型在多维德性上的伦理取向提供了可靠且可解释的框架，揭示了当前LLM在不同德性上的表现差异，有助于推动更符合人类价值观的AI系统设计。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often face ethical tradeoffs in which several responses may be defensible but express different priorities, such as fairness, honesty, courage, or restraint. We introduce VirtueMap, a framework for describing these patterns through an Aristotelian virtue-ethics lens. Instead of asking for a single correct answer, VirtueMap asks humans or LLMs to rank all five responses to each of seven general, non-lethal, non-political, and non-religious ethical dilemmas. To define the reference orderings used for scoring, we first proposed, for each dilemma and virtue, an ordering of the five responses from most to least expressive of that virtue. We then collected more than 100 respondent evaluations per ordering and retained it as operational ground truth only when at least 95% confirmed it. Rankings are scored against these retained orderings using normalized Borda alignment, yielding profiles over Practical Wisdom, Justice, Truthfulness, Courage, and Temperance. We apply VirtueMap to nine LLM families in a repeated-run evaluation and find high mean rank consistency (90.3%), with the largest differences appearing on Courage, Temperance, and Justice. We also release an interactive website that computes profiles locally in the browser and compares respondents with measured LLM profiles.

</details>


### [133] [An AI agent for treatment reasoning over a biomedical tool universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28692)
*Shanghua Gao,Ayush Noori,Richard Zhu,Curtis Ginder,Zhenglun Kong,Xiaorui Su,Justin Kauffman,Benjamin S. Glicksberg,Joshua Lampert,Ankit Sakhuja,Ashwin Sawant,ATHENA-R1 Evaluation Consortium,David A. Clifton,Noa Dagan,Ran Balicer,Marinka Zitnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ATHENA-R1是一种基于强化学习的AI代理，用于药物治疗推理，能够整合多种生物医学信息并利用212种工具进行迭代式证据收集，在多项任务中显著超越现有语言模型，准确率高达94.7%（药物推理）和82.9%（治疗推理），并获得临床专家认可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 治疗决策需要综合疾病背景、合并症、禁忌症和最新医学知识，传统AI系统难以实现具备可验证依据的迭代式推理，因此需要构建一个能自主寻求证据、动态调整方案的智能系统。

Method: 提出ATHENA-R1，一种面向自1939年以来所有FDA批准药物的治疗推理AI代理；采用多智能体系统自动生成工具、任务和推理轨迹，通过监督微调与科学反馈驱动的强化学习训练，每一步自主识别缺失信息、选择并运行相关生物医学工具以积累证据。

Result: 在3,168项药物推理任务和456例患者治疗案例中，ATHENA-R1分别达到94.7%和82.9%的准确率，比GPT-5高出17.8和10.7个百分点；在28个罕见病组织专家的盲评中全面优于基准模型，并在心血管和感染性疾病病例中获医生积极评价；其生成的不良事件假设在540万患者电子健康记录中得到验证（校正OR=1.48–1.84）。

Conclusion: 治疗推理可被重构为一种可通过强化学习掌握的迭代式证据收集过程，ATHENA-R1展示了AI在复杂、可解释临床决策中的潜力，为高风险医疗场景下的可信AI提供了新范式。

Abstract: Treatment reasoning underpins every therapeutic decision, integrating disease context, comorbidities, medications, contraindications, and evolving biomedical knowledge to select an appropriate therapy. It is inherently iterative: candidates are weighed against many constraints, revised as evidence emerges, and grounded in verifiable sources. Here we introduce ATHENA-R1, an AI agent for treatment reasoning across all FDA approved drugs since 1939, trained by reinforcement learning over a universe of 212 biomedical tools. At each step it identifies missing information, selects and runs relevant tools, and incorporates the evidence. To train it without human-annotated traces, we build a two-level self-learning framework: multi-agent systems construct the tools, tasks, and reasoning trajectories for supervised fine-tuning, then reinforcement learning with scientific feedback rewards reasoning quality (evidence gathering, grounded tool use, logical non-redundancy). Across five benchmarks of 3,168 drug reasoning tasks and 456 patient treatment cases, ATHENA-R1 outperforms language models and tool-use systems, reaching 94.7% accuracy on open-ended drug reasoning and 82.9% on treatment reasoning, 17.8 and 10.7 points above GPT-5. In blinded evaluations by experts from 28 rare disease organizations, it is preferred over reference models on all criteria, and physicians rated it favorably on complex hospitalized cardiovascular and infectious-disease cases. Adverse-event hypotheses it generated, tested in electronic health records from 5.4 million patients, reached adjusted odds ratios of 1.48-1.84, with no elevation among negative controls. Because it requires knowing what evidence to seek before concluding, treatment reasoning has long been hard for AI; we show it can be reframed as a learnable process of iterative evidence gathering that reinforcement learning can train AI to perform.

</details>


### [134] [COMPASS: Grounding Composition-Intent Guidance in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28696)
*Ziqi Zhou,Weize Quan,Mining Tan,Zhihan Chen,Dandan Zheng,Jingdong Chen,Jun Zhou,Weiming Dong,Dong-Ming Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了COMPASS，首个统一的多模态框架，通过共享专家令牌τ_c实现对构图意图的感知与生成控制，并构建了大规模数据集Comp-11用于评估，显著提升了构图理解和生成的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型在细粒度构图识别上不可靠，且难以将构图意图转化为可控生成，缺乏统一框架支持构图感知与生成的一体化控制。

Method: 提出COMPASS框架，在感知侧通过MoE架构注入构图专家知识并提取意图至共享专家令牌τ_c；在生成侧将τ_c作为全局条件信号引导去噪过程；同时构建含11类分类体系和推理增强标注的大规模数据集Comp-11。

Result: 实验表明COMPASS在类别级构图理解上显著优于强基线模型，在生成结果的构图一致性和提示忠实性方面表现更优。

Conclusion: COMPASS成功实现了从被动构图分析到主动布局控制的转化，为多模态模型中的构图意图建模提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Composition is a high-level visual intent that governs where subjects are placed and how a scene is organized, yet current unified multimodal models remain unreliable at fine-grained composition recognition and struggle to turn such intent into controllable generation. We present COMPASS, the first unified multimodal framework that grounds composition-intent control in a single system spanning both composition perception and composition-guided generation, with a shared expert token $τ_c$ as the central intent anchor. On the perception side, COMPASS injects composition expertise into an MoE backbone in a minimally invasive manner and distills the inferred intent into $τ_c$. On the generation side, COMPASS reuses $τ_c$ as a global conditioning signal that steers the denoising trajectory, effectively converting passive composition analysis into explicit layout control. To support systematic instruction-following composition learning and evaluation at scale, we construct Comp-11, a large-scale dataset with an 11-class taxonomy and reasoning-augmented annotations. Extensive experiments show that COMPASS substantially improves category-level composition understanding and delivers more composition-consistent, prompt-faithful generation than strong baselines.

</details>


### [135] [BV-Blend: Uncertainty-Weighted Historical Baselines for Stable Critic-Free RL with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28707)
*Yupeng Chang,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为BV-Blend的无批评者强化学习框架，通过结合语义聚类的历史统计信息和提示局部的即时统计信息来稳定优势估计，从而提升大语言模型对齐训练的稳定性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GRPO方法依赖于提示组内的奖励统计，当所有 rollout 获得相同奖励时，组内方差为零，导致优势估计失效，尤其在二值验证器的冷启动阶段学习受阻。

Method: BV-Blend维护每个语义簇的历史奖励矩（均值和方差）的指数移动平均，并基于标准误差代理计算置信权重，将历史统计与当前提示组的局部统计加权融合，用于标准化优势估计，支持PPO式的截断更新。

Result: 在可验证推理基准上的实验表明，BV-Blend相比基于组归一化的方法具有更好的训练稳定性和更高的性能，且在奖励方差为零等挑战性场景下仍保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: BV-Blend通过引入语义感知的历史信息融合机制，有效解决了GRPO类方法在冷启动和低方差情况下的训练停滞问题，为无批评者的强化学习提供了更稳健的替代方案。

Abstract: Critic-free reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), exemplified by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), avoids training a value function (critic) and reduces memory and compute overhead relative to critic-based PPO pipelines for aligning large language models. However, GRPO-style advantage estimation depends on prompt-local (within-prompt-group) reward statistics and can be unstable. In particular, when all rollouts in a prompt group receive identical rewards, the within-group reward variance becomes zero, and group normalization yields zero advantages for that group, impeding learning in cold-start regimes with binary verifiers. We introduce BV-Blend, a critic-free framework that stabilizes advantage estimation by combining prompt-local on-policy statistics with semantic-cluster-conditioned historical moments. BV-Blend maintains EMA-tracked reward moments for each cluster, derives a confidence weight from a standard error of the mean (SEM) proxy, and uses this weight to blend historical and prompt-local baseline and variance statistics into a standardized advantage for PPO-style clipped updates. Experiments on verifiable reasoning benchmarks show that BV-Blend improves training stability and performance, and remains robust in regimes where group-normalized methods may stall.

</details>


### [136] [The Two Genie Game: Adoption and Welfare in Audit-Grounded AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28710)
*Darrell Lewis-Sandy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在竞争市场中，一个以最小化伤害为目标的代理如何取代追求认可的代理，并探讨该策略在何种条件下能有效防止社区危害。通过演化博弈论建模，发现当对社区情感敏感度的先验分布具有单调性、端点反转和中心对称特性时，采用伤害最小化策略更可能成功固定下来，但前提是社区信息规模足够小以避免资源耗尽。然而，自我审计机制并不总能防止伤害，其效果依赖于代理与社区价值观的一致性及伤害评估的时间范围。一旦该策略占据主导，系统将陷入吸收状态，可能导致长期福利损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在负和环境中，基于审核的伤害最小化代理是否能在市场竞争中取代传统追求认可的代理，并分析其对社区长期安全的影响，揭示现有RLHF方法的潜在局限。

Method: 采用有限种群的演化博弈模型（Moran-Fermi成对比较），结合愿望者后见、同伴证言、单调伤害账本、充分的信息密度和有限资源池等假设，形式化分析代理采纳动态；使用长尾先验分布进行验证，并通过Lean 4对代数结构和网格计算进行机器验证。

Result: 证明了当先验分布满足单调性、端点反转和中心对称配对性质时，伤害最小化代理的采纳更受青睐；存在临界采纳水平，超过该水平则趋向固定；导出了社区有效信息规模N_c的上限，以确保在资源耗尽前实现固定；机器验证支持主要结论。

Conclusion: 伤害最小化策略虽在特定条件下可取代认可驱动代理并实现固定，但自我审计不足以普遍防止社区危害；其效果取决于价值对齐程度和时间范围，且一旦主导即形成吸收态，可能锁定延迟显现的伤害，导致长期负面后果。

Abstract: We ask under what conditions an agent with a harm-minimizing policy can displace an approval-seeking (RLHF) agent in a competitive market, and when that policy is sufficient to prevent community harm. We use evolutionary game theory (finite-population Moran-Fermi pairwise comparison) to formalize this subject to assumptions of wisher hindsight, peer testimony, a monotone harm ledger, sufficient information density of community feedback, and a finite, depleting resource pool, in a negative-sum environment.
  We show that adoption is favored when the prior distributions on how readily wishers attune to community sentiment are monotone, exhibit endpoint inversion, and have a centro-symmetric pairing property, and demonstrate this with several long-tailed priors (Hill, Pareto, Lomax, Frechet). Where it is favored, a critical adoption level separates communities that drift back to the approval-seeking agent from those for which the audited agent fixes; above that level fixation is the overwhelmingly likely outcome. We derive when fixation is attainable as a bound on the effective (informational) size N_c of the community, which must be small enough to allow fixation before depletion. We present these as Theorems 5.4 and 5.5; the algebraic and finite-grid backbone is machine-checked in Lean 4, with the barrier-crossing asymptotics retained as explicit hypotheses.
  We show that a self-audited agent with a community ledger is not, in general, sufficient to prevent community harm. Sufficiency depends both upon the alignment of the agent's audit with community values and the timeframe over which harm is evaluated. Regardless of alignment, once adoption reaches dominance, the state is absorbing. The same policy that reduced harm under alignment becomes a trap, welfare-negative under misalignment and, even under alignment, one that locks in harm deferred past the adoption horizon.

</details>


### [137] [TrajRS: Towards Certified Robustness in Pedestrian Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28716)
*Liang Zhang,Gaojie Jin,Yao Shi,Quanzhi Li,Cheng-Chao Huang,David N. Jansen,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的随机平滑框架TrajRS，用于为轨迹预测模型提供可验证的鲁棒性保证，特别是在面对对抗性攻击时确保行人轨迹预测的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的启发式防御策略在面对复杂的针对性对抗攻击时往往失效，因此需要建立可验证的安全保障机制来提升轨迹预测模型的鲁棒性。

Method: 扩展了传统的随机平滑框架，提出了名为TrajRS的方法，并针对“最优预测的鲁棒性”和“所有可能预测的鲁棒性”两种形式化定义设计了具体的实施方案。

Result: 实验结果表明，TrajRS能够有效为所有经过平滑处理的行人轨迹预测模型提供鲁棒性认证，显著提升了模型在对抗攻击下的可靠性。

Conclusion: TrajRS为自动驾驶中的轨迹预测模型提供了可验证的鲁棒性保障，增强了系统在复杂攻击场景下的安全性与可信度。

Abstract: The robustness of trajectory prediction models is crucial for developing safe autonomous driving systems. Adversarial attacks on trajectory prediction can significantly impair the accuracy of predicted trajectories, leading to hazardous driving behaviors. While heuristic defense strategies have been implemented to enhance the robustness of trajectory prediction models, these measures often fail against more sophisticated, targeted adversarial attacks. Hence, there is a pressing need to establish verifiable safety assurances for trajectory prediction models. In this paper, we extend the traditional Randomized Smoothing framework to "TrajRS", which provides a certified robust radius for smoothed trajectory predictors. We clarify and expand the formal definitions of robustness in trajectory prediction and tailor the practical TrajRS scheme specifically to "robustness for the optimal prediction" and "robustness for all possible predictions". An extensive set of experiments demonstrates that TrajRS effectively achieves robustness certification for all smoothed pedestrian trajectory predictors in this work.

</details>


### [138] [ComMem: Complementary Memory Systems for Test-Time Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28719)
*Guanglong Sun,Shuang Cui,Bo Lei,Liyuan Wang,Zihan Zhai,Hongwei Yan,Hang Su,Jun Zhu,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ComMem的新方法，通过模拟生物大脑中的互补记忆系统，实现视觉-语言模型在测试时的有效自适应，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时自适应方法通常局限于局部调整或单模态操作，未能充分利用视觉-语言模型的多模态特性，且缺乏长期知识积累。

Method: 设计了两个协同工作的记忆模块：类似海马体的快速适应详细记忆，用于构建高置信度测试样本的动态视觉缓存；以及类似新皮层的慢速整合抽象记忆，用于持续优化全局文本原型，并通过联合优化确保跨模态一致性。

Result: 在15个基准数据集上的实验表明，ComMem在自然分布偏移和跨数据集泛化场景下均显著优于当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: ComMem为提升视觉-语言模型在动态真实环境中的实用性提供了有效且有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) of vision-language models (VLMs) is essential for their robust deployment in dynamic, real-world environments. However, existing TTA methods often adapt locally without accumulating knowledge over time, or operating within a single modality without exploiting VLMs' inherently multi-modal nature. Inspired by the \textbf{Com}plementary \textbf{Mem}ory systems of the biological brain, we propose \textbf{ComMem}, an innovative approach that mimics the distinct but cooperative roles of the hippocampus and neocortex to enable effective TTA for VLMs. ComMem consists of two key components: a fast-adapting detailed memory, akin to the hippocampus, that forms a dynamic visual cache from high-confidence test samples; and a slow-integrating abstract memory, akin to the neocortex, that continually refines global textual prototypes. For each test instance, ComMem jointly optimizes both memory systems to ensure cross-modal consistency. Extensive experiments on 15 benchmark datasets show that ComMem significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods under both natural distribution shifts and cross-dataset generalization, offering a promising direction for enhancing VLMs' practical adaptability.

</details>


### [139] [Agentic Abstention: Do Agents Know When to Stop Instead of Act?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28733)
*Han Luo,Bingbing Wen,Lucy Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“代理 abstention”问题，即在不确定性下决定智能体何时应停止行动。研究发现，不同规模和能力的模型在及时 abstention 上表现不一，且任务环境会显著影响决策时机。为此，作者提出 CONVOLVE 方法，通过上下文工程从交互轨迹中提取可重用的停止规则，在不更新模型参数的情况下显著提升了及时 abstention 能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM代理通常无法判断何时应停止尝试完成任务，尤其是在目标不明确或环境不可达时仍持续调用工具，导致效率低下。因此需要研究代理在多轮交互中何时应主动放弃任务的问题。

Method: 定义了“agentic abstention”这一序列决策问题，并在网页购物、终端环境和问答任务中评估了13种LLM代理系统和2种代理框架的表现；提出CONVOLVE方法，通过上下文工程将完整交互轨迹提炼为可复用的停止规则，以提升及时停止的能力。

Result: 实验表明现有代理在是否以及何时停止方面存在显著差距，部分代理从不及时停止，部分在多次无效交互后才停止；更大的模型不一定表现更好；CONVOLVE方法在WebShop数据集上将Llama-3.3-70B的及时召回率从26.7提升至57.4。

Conclusion: 及时的代理 abstention 是一个关键但被忽视的问题，仅能推理或更大规模的模型并不意味着更好的停止判断能力；通过上下文工程提取停止规则（如CONVOLVE）可在不微调模型的情况下显著改善代理的停止决策行为。

Abstract: LLM agents are expected to act over multiple turns, using search, browsing interfaces, and terminal tools to complete user goals. Yet not every goal is well specified or achievable in the available environment. In such cases, a reliable agent should recognize that further interaction is unlikely to help and abstain from additional tool calls. We define Agentic Abstention, the problem of deciding when an agent should stop acting under uncertainty. Unlike standard LLM abstention, which is usually evaluated as a single-turn answer-or-abstain decision, agentic abstention is a sequential decision problem: an agent can answer, abstain, or gather more information at each turn, and the need to abstain may only become clear after interacting with the environment. We study this problem across web shopping, terminal environments, and question answering, evaluating 13 LLM-as-agent systems and 2 agent scaffolds on more than 28,000 tasks. Our results show that the main challenge is not only whether agents can abstain, but also when they abstain. Some agents never abstain when they should, while others do so only after many unnecessary interactions. This gap is especially large on tasks where the instruction appears feasible until the environment reveals otherwise (e.g., no valid result matches the instruction). We further find that model scale, reasoning, and agent scaffolding affect abstention in different ways, where larger or more capable models sometimes perform worse at timely abstention. Finally, we introduce CONVOLVE, a context engineering method for improving agentic abstention that distills full interaction trajectories into reusable stopping rules. On WebShop, CONVOLVE substantially improves timely abstention without updating model parameters, raising Llama-3.3-70B's timely recall rate from 26.7 to 57.4. Our dataset and code are available at https://lhannnn.github.io/agentic-abstention

</details>


### [140] [Agent Safety Is Action Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28739)
*Shawn Li,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为将聊天机器人时代的内容安全方法（如拒绝响应）直接应用于代理型大语言模型是一种类别错误，因为代理行为的安全风险本质上不同于内容输出风险。文章指出，行为安全无法通过模型权重学习来保证，而应通过外部机制在行动边界上实施最小权限原则，并以“行动对齐”这一关系性属性进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型越来越多地作为代理执行现实世界中的操作（如转账、删除记录等），传统的基于拒绝机制的内容安全策略被沿用，但作者认为这种做法忽视了代理行为中权限滥用的本质风险，因而需要重新定义安全范式。

Method: 通过三个跨越自主性谱系的实证线索进行论证：防御训练模型仅学习表面模式而非真实意图；相同训练导致多步代理在无威胁时即崩溃但仍可被利用；甚至未加防御的前沿模型在正常使用下也会超出用户授权。

Result: 发现当前基于拒绝的安全训练无法有效防止代理越权行为，反而损害模型能力并提供虚假安全性；表面防御容易被绕过，且模型在正常操作中也会越权。

Conclusion: 行动安全不能内化于模型参数中，必须在模型外部、在执行动作的边界上通过最小权限原则强制执行，并以部署条件下的关系性‘行动对齐’而非简单的拒绝率来评估安全性。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly act as agents: they call tools, move money, delete records, and send messages on a user's behalf. To keep them safe, practitioners imported the chatbot-era recipe (train the model to refuse unsafe inputs) into the agentic setting, and treat the resulting capability loss as a manageable ``alignment tax.'' We argue this is a \emph{category error}. Refusal is a primitive for \emph{content safety}, where the harm is in the model's output and is therefore a learnable function of it. Agentic harm is different in kind: it lies not in any output but in the relation between the authority an action exercises and the authority the user granted, which is absent from the text the model sees. Importing content-safety methods into this regime does not trade capability for safety; it pays capability and buys negative security. We support this with three lines of evidence spanning the autonomy spectrum: defense-trained models learn surface patterns rather than intent; the same training collapses multi-step agents before any threat appears while leaving them exploitable; and even undefended frontier models exceed granted authority under ordinary use. We conclude that action safety cannot be installed in weights. It must be expressed as \emph{least privilege}, enforced \emph{outside} the model at the action boundary, and evaluated as \emph{action alignment} (a relational, deployment-conditioned property) rather than a refusal score.

</details>


### [141] [Self-Supervised Theorem Discovery in a Formal Axiomatic System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28747)
*Kazuki Ota,Takayuki Osa,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种自监督的定理发现算法，通过交替进行证明搜索和有用定理提取，能够在没有人类先验知识的情况下自主发现大量有意义的定理，并提升大语言模型在数学推理中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在没有人类提供的数学文本、代码或定理库等先验知识的情况下，智能体是否可以自主发现有用的数学定理。

Method: 开发了一个仅从公理和推理规则出发的智能体，采用自监督的定理发现算法，交替进行证明搜索与有用定理提取，逐步构建可复用为引理的定理库。

Result: 该智能体发现了数万个定理，能够解决人类编写的基准问题，并且这些发现的定理作为提示词可提升大语言模型的证明性能。

Conclusion: 无需依赖人类提供的定理库，仅通过证明搜索即可涌现出对数学推理有意义的定理，为可验证的自进化AI数学系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent artificial intelligence (AI) systems have shown remarkable progress in mathematical reasoning. Many existing approaches, including large language models (LLMs), draw on human prior knowledge in the form of mathematical text, code, or theorem libraries. Although these approaches are highly effective in practice, it remains an open question whether an agent can autonomously discover useful theorems without such human priors. We study this question in a formal axiomatic system by developing an agent that starts from axioms and inference rules alone and gradually grows a library of useful theorems. Concretely, we propose a self-supervised theorem-discovery algorithm that alternates between proof search and useful-theorem extraction, building a theorem library whose entries are reused as lemmas for subsequent proof search. Experiments show that the agent discovers tens of thousands of theorems and finds proofs for human-written benchmark problems, suggesting that its discoveries include theorems meaningful from a human mathematical perspective. Furthermore, the discovered theorems improve LLM proof performance when provided as prompt lemmas, indicating that they can serve as external knowledge for LLM reasoning. Our results provide evidence that useful theorems can emerge from proof search without relying on human-provided theorem libraries. More broadly, they suggest a path toward self-evolving AI systems for mathematics whose discoveries remain formally verifiable.

</details>


### [142] [Mechanistic Personality Analysis of LLMs Steering Personality via Latent Feature Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28770)
*David Courtis,Ting Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于机制可解释性的方法，通过稀疏自编码器和对比激活分析，在残差流中识别与OCEAN人格特质相关的潜在方向，并通过添加激活空间中的微小偏移来增强目标人格特质，同时保持语言建模性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖提示工程或微调来塑造大模型的人格特质，缺乏对模型内部机制的理解和直接干预手段。

Method: 利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）和对比激活分析识别与目标OCEAN特质相关的潜在方向，构建激活空间中的加性引导向量，并通过线性加权启发式与网格搜索优化确定最优特征偏移组合。

Result: 能够在保持标准基准性能的同时，有效且可控地调节大语言模型生成文本中的人格特质表达。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了在机制层面直接干预大语言模型人格特质的生成，为可控的人格化文本生成提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated the ability to simulate human-like OCEAN personality traits in generated text. Previous efforts have focused on prompt engineering or fine-tuning to shape LLM personality. In this work, we propose a mechanistic interpretability approach that directly intervenes on the model's latent features. Our method identifies latent directions in the residual stream corresponding to a target OCEAN trait using sparse autoencoders (SAEs) and contrastive activation analysis. We formalize an additive steering vector in activation space and demonstrate how applying a small additive shift to the hidden states enhances the target trait while preserving overall language modeling performance. To determine the optimal combination of feature shifts, we explore a linear weighting heuristic with grid search optimization that balances personality expression with task performance. Our approach shows promise in controllably steering personality traits at the mechanistic level while maintaining high performance on standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [143] [HyphaeDB: A Living Knowledge Topology for Agent-First Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28781)
*Krishna Halaharvi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyphaeDB 是一种面向多智能体系统的新型内存基础设施，它将向量数据库中的 HNSW 图结构重新定义为知识传播的通信网络，通过基于拓扑的八卦协议实现智能体间的自动知识传递与共识形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量数据库和智能体记忆框架仅将记忆视为被动存储，缺乏通过内存层在智能体间主动传播知识的机制，限制了多智能体系统的协同效率与涌现能力。

Method: 提出 HyphaeDB 架构，以 HNSW 图拓扑作为通信基础，引入三个核心原语（知识节点、拓扑边、内存差异），结合能量衰减的八卦传播协议和基于局部规则的交互机制，并构建多层抽象层级实现共识驱动的知识提升。

Result: 实现了基于 PostgreSQL 和 pgvector 的参考系统，在 Swarm-Driven Development 方法论中完成实际部署，验证了矛盾检测、模式结晶和共识形成等涌现行为的有效性。

Conclusion: HyphaeDB 首次将可导航小世界拓扑与基于八卦的知识传播相结合，为多智能体系统提供了具备自组织与协作能力的原生记忆架构新范式。

Abstract: Every existing vector database and agent memory framework treats memory as passive storage that agents query explicitly. No system propagates knowledge between agents through the memory layer itself. We introduce HyphaeDB, an agent-native memory infrastructure that reinterprets the Hierarchical Navigable Small World (HNSW) graph topology the data structure at the core of every modern vector database not as a search optimization, but as a communication fabric for multi-agent AI systems. In HyphaeDB, agents are nodes in the vector space with persistent positions, knowledge propagates via a gossip protocol through the graph's neighbor structure with energy-based attenuation, and emergent behaviors contradiction detection, pattern crystallization, and consensus formation arise from the combination of topology, propagation dynamics, and local interaction rules. We present the architecture built on three primitives (knowledge nodes, topology edges, and memory diffs), a multi-layer abstraction hierarchy with promotion via emergent consensus, and theoretical analysis grounding the system in small-world network theory, epidemic broadcast protocols, and swarm intelligence. We provide a reference implementation on PostgreSQL with pgvector and describe a concrete deployment in Swarm-Driven Development, a multi-agent software engineering methodology. HyphaeDB represents, to our knowledge, the first system to combine navigable small world topology with gossip-based knowledge propagation for multi-agent coordination.

</details>


### [144] [Primary ICD Category Prediction using LLM-based Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28798)
*Chengyuan Liu,Xinyue Zhang,Yao Li,Guanting Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了利用冻结的医学大语言模型（LLM）表征作为共享嵌入空间，实现基于结构化变量和临床文本的多模态主要诊断类别预测。在MIMIC-IV数据集上验证表明，结合两种模态的线性探针性能最优，且小参数适配器可实现跨数据集迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化编码系统难以有效整合来自临床文本和结构化电子健康记录（EHR）的诊断信息，限制了ICD编码的准确性和应用效率。因此，需要一种能够统一多模态EHR数据的表示方法以提升诊断预测性能。

Method: 构建包含13,645次住院的MIMIC-IV队列，将前10个最常见ICD-10代码归为七类。将结构化变量序列化为临床叙述，并与去泄漏的出院记录结合。使用冻结的MedFound-Llama3-8B微调模型提取五层Transformer的隐藏状态，训练用于单一模态和联合输入的线性探针，并与XGBoost及PLM-ICD基线比较；同时在MIMIC-III上评估带小型瓶颈适配器的跨数据集迁移能力。

Result: 联合探针在MIMIC-IV上表现最佳（严格准确率87.69%，医学准确率91.45%），优于单模态探针和基线模型；仅结构化探针的医学准确率比基线高6.19个百分点；深层隐藏状态中诊断信息线性可分性增强；仅用5%标签的200万参数适配器即可恢复MIMIC-III上的跨数据集迁移性能。

Conclusion: 对冻结医学大语言模型表征进行多模态探针分析，是一种研究EHR不同模态及跨数据集适应临床表征的有效且实用的方法。

Abstract: Objective: ICD codes are central to reimbursement, research, and population health surveillance, yet automated coding systems often struggle to integrate diagnostic signals from both clinical narratives and structured electronic health record (EHR) variables. We evaluated whether frozen medical large language model (LLM) representations can serve as a shared embedding space for multimodal primary diagnosis category prediction.
  Materials and Methods: We constructed a MIMIC-IV cohort of 13,645 admissions from the 10 most frequent primary ICD-10 codes, consolidated into seven categories. Structured variables were serialized into clinical narratives and combined with leakage-pruned discharge notes. Using a frozen MedFound-Llama3-8B-finetuned backbone, we extracted hidden states from five transformer layers and trained linear probes for structured-only, unstructured-only, and combined inputs, comparing against XGBoost and information-matched PLM-ICD baselines and evaluating MIMIC-III adaptation with a compact bottleneck adapter.
  Results: The combined probe performed best on MIMIC-IV (87.69% strict; 91.45% medical accuracy), exceeding both single-modality probes and baselines. The structured-only probe outperformed its standard baseline by 6.19 points in medical accuracy. Diagnostic information became increasingly linearly separable in deeper layers, and a 2M-parameter adapter restored cross-dataset transfer to MIMIC-III using only 5% of target labels.
  Discussion: LLM embeddings can unify structured and narrative EHR information for multimodal diagnosis prediction, supporting efficient reuse of clinical representations across modalities and datasets through a small representation-level module.
  Conclusion: Multimodal probing of frozen medical LLM representations provides a practical approach for studying EHR modalities and adapting clinical representations across datasets.

</details>


### [145] [MedEvoEval: Evaluating Continual Evolution of Doctor Agents through Simulated Clinical Episodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28900)
*Hui Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedEvoEval是一个基于动作触发的模拟门诊病程的可执行纵向评估框架，用于全面评估医生智能体在跨病程经验积累下的临床决策能力演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学问答和交互式评估基准无法充分覆盖医生智能体在多病程中通过记忆、检索、反思等机制演化的复杂决策过程，缺乏对跨病程经验与单病程决策交互的系统性评估。

Method: 提出MedEvoEval框架，将每个源病例转化为角色特定的患者、检查和管理视图；证据仅通过有效动作逐步揭示；每段病程记录包含观察、行动、输出、评分及可选经验写回的结构化轨迹；并发布包含700个病程及相关工具的可运行评估包。

Result: 实验表明，该框架的病程轨迹能揭示仅看最终答案所掩盖的过程成本，展示MDT式会诊如何重新分配资源，并支持对记忆成熟、保留能力、迁移表现和更新响应等的纵向分析。

Conclusion: MedEvoEval为评估医生智能体是否随经验改进、能否迁移有用行为以及长期保持早期能力提供了具体可行的基础。

Abstract: Doctor agents are moving beyond single-turn answer generation toward evolving clinical decision systems. Within an outpatient episode, they acquire evidence, use examination and consultation resources, and decide when to finalize a diagnosis and management plan. Across episodes, their behavior may change through memory, retrieval, reflection, or other update mechanisms. Current evaluations only partially cover this setting. Fixed-input medical QA benchmarks score final answers from complete inputs, whereas many interactive benchmarks still focus on individual encounters or fixed runs, providing limited support for evaluating how episode-level decisions interact with cross-episode experience. We introduce MedEvoEval, an executable longitudinal evaluation framework based on action-gated simulated outpatient episodes. Each source case is converted into role-specific patient, examination, and manager views; evidence is revealed only through valid actions; and each episode records a structured trace that links observations, actions, final outputs, manager scores, and optional experience write-back. We release a runnable E&D artifact with 700 processed episodes, provenance notes, schemas, an episode runner, scoring scripts, configurations, example logs, analysis code, and trajectory- and step-level derivatives. Experiments show that episode traces expose process costs hidden by final-answer scoring, show how MDT-style consultation reallocates resources, and support longitudinal analyses of memory maturation, held-out transfer, update-stage response, and backward retention. Together, these results show that MedEvoEval provides a concrete basis for evaluating whether doctor agents improve through experience, transfer useful behavior, and retain earlier capabilities over time.

</details>


### [146] [Expert Evaluation of Clinical AI Tools on Real Point-of-Care Clinical Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28960)
*Jean Feng,Vishal Patel,Patrick Heagerty,Yifan Mai,Venkatesh Sivaraman,Patrick Vossler,Jialin Ouyang,Anupam B. Jena*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究基于620个真实临床场景中的临床问题（Real-POCQi）和187个HealthBench问题，评估了三种通用大模型（Claude Opus 4.8、Gemini 3.1 Pro、GPT-5.5）与专用临床工具OpenEvidence（OE）的表现。149名来自36个州的执业医师进行了双盲对比评分，结果显示OE在准确性、临床实用性、来源质量、可验证性和完整性五个维度上均显著优于通用模型，胜率差达25至39个百分点。结果在多种敏感性分析中保持稳定。研究还发现LLM评判者与专家评判者存在系统性差异。研究强调应使用真实世界问题和专科专家进行AI工具评估，并指出专用工具的优势凸显了定制化工程对提升临床AI性能的重要性。作者公开了Real-POCQi基准和预设统计分析代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI工具多基于假设性或考试类问题进行评估，未能反映临床实践中真实提出的问题。为弥补这一差距，本研究旨在通过真实世界中医生在诊疗现场提出的临床问题，客观评估通用大模型与专用临床AI工具的实际表现差异。

Method: 构建包含620个真实诊疗点查询（Real-POCQi）和187个HealthBench问题的数据集，在OpenEvidence平台上进行双盲评估；邀请149名来自30个专科的执业医师按专业匹配问题，对四种AI工具的回答在五个临床相关维度（准确性、临床实用性、来源质量、可验证性、完整性）进行头对头比较；同时分析LLM评判者与专家的一致性，并进行多项敏感性分析（如引文显示、回答长度、用户状态等）。

Result: 在Real-POCQi主分析中，专用临床工具OpenEvidence在所有五个评估维度上的胜率差（赢-输）比三个前沿通用大模型高出25至39个百分点（p<0.001），优势显著且在各类敏感性分析中保持一致；LLM评判者与专家评判者存在系统性差异，尽管两者对最佳模型的判断总体一致。

Conclusion: AI临床工具的评估应基于真实世界的查询分布，并由与问题专业匹配的专家进行评判；专用工具相较于通用模型的持续优势表明，针对性的工程优化和定制化设计能显著提升AI在临床决策支持中的实际性能。

Abstract: Physicians now pose millions of clinical questions to AI tools each week, yet these tools are evaluated largely on hypothetical or exam-style questions, not those actually asked in practice. We report a blinded evaluation built on 620 Real-world Point-Of-Care Queries (Real-POCQi) submitted to the OpenEvidence (OE) platform by physicians spanning 30 specialties, as well as 187 questions from HealthBench. 149 practicing physicians across 36 states made head-to-head comparisons between answers from three frontier general-purpose models (Claude Opus 4.8, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and GPT-5.5) and a specialized clinical tool (OE), with graders matched to each question's specialty. When comparing answers along five dimensions relevant to clinical decision support -- accuracy, clinical utility, source quality, verifiability, & completeness -- physicians scored the specialized tool highest on all axes; in the primary analysis on Real-POCQi, win differences (margins between win and loss rates) ranged from 25 to 39 percentage points (p<0.001). Results remained consistent in sensitivity analyses stratifying by citation display, answer length, OE-user status, and Real-POCQi versus HealthBench. In parallel, LLM judges were found to systematically differ from expert judges, though both generally agreed on the best model. These findings underscore two conclusions: (i) AI tool evaluations should reflect real-world query distributions and use expert judges that mirror the specialization defining modern medicine and (ii) the consistent advantage of the specialized tool over general-purpose models does not necessarily mean that the latter cannot serve similar purposes, but that targeted engineering and customization can yield meaningful gains in performance for its users. We release Real-POCQi as a public benchmark, as well as the prespecified statistical analysis for reproducing results of this study.

</details>


### [147] [Customized Generative AI Agent for Transportation Engineering Practice: A Development and Continued Pre-training Guideline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29014)
*Dianwei Chen,Yuan-Zheng Lei,Zifan Zhang,Yuchen Liu,Xianfeng,Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于LoRA的持续预训练方法，用于构建面向交通工程领域的定制化生成式AI代理，通过专业文档微调多个大语言模型，显著提升了其在技术语义理解和领域推理中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型因缺乏对工程术语、技术标准和领域语义的理解，在专业工程应用中效果受限，因此需要开发针对交通工程领域的专用AI模型。

Method: 采用美国交通手册、设计规范和法规文件构建专业语料库，并基于统一的低秩适应（LoRA）框架对六种最先进的大语言模型进行持续预训练。

Result: 使用BLEU-4和ROUGE等自然语言处理指标评估显示，Qwen2.5-7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B在领域对齐性和响应质量方面表现最佳，验证了LoRA适配在技术内容理解中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个可复现的领域专用生成式AI代理开发框架，有助于推动生成式AI在交通研究、设计、规划和政策分析中的广泛应用。

Abstract: Recent advancements in generative artificial intelligence (AI) and large language models (LLMs) have shown significant promise in automating complex reasoning, summarization, and question-answering tasks. However, the effectiveness of general-purpose LLMs in specialized engineering domains remains limited due to insufficient exposure to technical standards, engineering terminology, and domain-specific semantics. This study proposes a systematic approach to developing a customized generative AI agent for transportation engineering applications. A curated corpus of U.S. transportation manuals, design guidelines, and regulatory documents is used to conduct continued pretraining of six state-of-the-art LLMs through a unified low-rank adaptation (LoRA) framework. The training process is monitored to ensure convergence and model stability. Performance is evaluated using standard natural language processing metrics, including BLEU-4 and ROUGE, with Qwen2.5-7B and LLaMA-3.1-8B demonstrating the highest domain alignment and response quality. Results validate the effectiveness of LoRA-based adaptation in improving LLM performance on technical content interpretation and context-specific reasoning. This work contributes a reproducible development framework for constructing domain-specialized generative AI agents, supporting broader deployment in transportation research, design, planning, and policy analysis.

</details>


### [148] [Preventing Error Propagation in Multi-Agent AI through Runtime Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29026)
*Shahnewaz Karim Sakib,Anindya Bijoy Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多智能体AI系统在推理交换和运行时答案修订中的可靠性，提出了一种框架，通过独立回答、共享推理路径并修改决策来评估多智能体协作的效果，并分析其在网络安全、网络通信和常识等领域的准确性和错误传播风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体AI系统在协作过程中可能因推理交流纠正错误，但也可能导致原本正确的答案被误导，因此需要研究其通信的可靠性。

Method: 构建一个框架，智能体首先独立回答选择题，然后共享推理路径并修订决策，通过数值实验评估准确性提升、正向与负向答案转变的比例及其跨领域有效性。

Result: 实验表明多智能体推理在某些情况下能提高可靠性，但在其他情况下也可能传播错误，效果因领域而异。

Conclusion: 多智能体AI系统的推理交换可以提升性能，但其可靠性取决于具体应用场景，需谨慎设计以避免错误传播。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI systems can improve answer selection by allowing different language models to exchange reasoning traces, revise initial predictions, and support a final decision. However, such communication may also introduce reliability risks: reasoning from one agent can correct another agent's mistake, but it can also mislead an agent that was initially correct. This paper studies reliable multi-agent AI communication through reasoning exchange and runtime answer revision.
